It just slips away.\n\nDoctor: Do you often feel restless or fidgety?\n\nPatient: I don't know about fidgety. I just... I've been doing a lot. Like, I had to get everything moving all at once. I completely missed a deadline for a big custom piece. Everything just got so disorganized that I... I had to stop taking new orders for a bit.\n\nDoctor: Have you been feeling anxious or stressed out lately?\n\nPatient: Um... no, not really. I don't... I wouldn't say I feel anxious. I don't... I wouldn't call it distress. It's just... stuff that's happening. I'm fine.\n\nDoctor: Have you had to cut back on any activities or responsibilities because of these problems with focus and restlessness?\n\nPatient: Um... yeah, I guess a little. I... I missed a deadline for a big custom piece. And my shop records are just... a mess right now. So I had to stop taking new orders. Just for a bit. My family's been calling more, too. I think they noticed I wasn't really doing my normal stuff. But it's fine.\n\nDoctor: Are you having trouble keeping up with your work or daily responsibilities because of your difficulties with focus and restlessness?\n\nPatient: I mean... like I just said. With the shop, and missing that deadline. That's my work. But other than that... no, I don't think so. Just what I told you.\n\nDoctor: Have you been acting on impulse more often than usual?\n\nPatient: Um... no. Not really. I don't think I've been doing anything like that. Just... normal stuff.\n\nDoctor: Are you finding it difficult to manage your daily life because of these problems with focus and restlessness?\n\nPatient: I...
